A scale-down's own dropped servers, terminating at or above the spec, are not these.
- **why:** #619 S1, the v1.24.0 journey (3 × 2026.08.1, 2026-10-29), and the same on v1.23.0: 5 → 3, "Scale-down drain complete", and about 2 s later back to 5. The StatefulSet created server-3 and server-4 again on their old volumes, which the collector had not deleted yet (the removed pods were still terminating; a volume whose pod is in range again is retained), the servers started with the identities Neo4j had dropped, and the cluster stayed `Forming` ("Cluster formation verification incomplete") for 4 minutes and more, with `Degraded=False` "All 3 servers are available" and no condition or event saying why. Lowering `servers` again recovered it. The docs of v1.24.0 warned about it (#620).
- **pinned-by:** `TestHoldRaise_WaitsForTheRemovedServersPodsAndVolumes` (server-3's pod terminating, both volumes owned by their pods: held at 3, the condition, one `ScaleUpDeferred`, the 5 s wake; server-3's pod and volume gone: 4; server-4's volume gone: 5 and `False`), `TestHoldRaise_ANormalScaleUpIsNotHeld` (nothing left; a volume nothing reclaims), `TestHoldRaise_ReadsTheReplicasFromTheAPIServer` (a cache behind in either direction), `TestHoldRaise_WhatOutlastsTheGraceIsNamed`, `TestReconcileServerShortfall_DroppedServersInKeptPodsAreNamed`, `TestDroppedServersInKeptPods` (`internal/controller/raise_hold_test.go`); the envtest spec "keeps the replicas until the volume is gone, then adds the servers" (`internal/controller/raise_hold_envtest_test.go`: through `Reconcile`, the StatefulSet stays at 3 replicas with a removed server's volume there, the result asks to run again within 5 s, and goes to 5 once it is gone); `TestPassWakes_BringTheNextPassForward` (`internal/controller/scale_down_settle_test.go`); `TestHoldTopologyWhileUpgradeHolds` (`internal/controller/upgrade_topology_unit_test.go`, through `holdTopologyChange`). Each part switched off under `go test -overlay` fails its tests: the hold (the first and the grace test; the envtest spec: 5 replicas), the replicas read from the cache, the reclaimed-volume test (a volume made ready held for), the grace, a pod's deletion deadline worded as when it began to terminate (the grace test), the `False` message after the wait (the first test: the upgrade's message back, or not kept on the next pass), the stuck report, its readiness check (a new server starting while the old row lingers named), and the wake `Reconcile` applies (the envtest spec: 30 s).
- **enforcement:** unit test + envtest; not walked live yet.

### id 230 — ScaledWithoutRestart names only the servers a scale adds
- **scope:** `serversAdded` and the scale branch of `ReconcileConfigMap` (`internal/controller/configmap_manager.go`); the `ScaledWithoutRestart` row of `docs/user_guide/guides/kubernetes-events.md`.
- **rule:** A ConfigMap rewrite whose only change is the discovery list (#467) announces `ScaledWithoutRestart` and logs "Adding servers 3-4 without restarting the running servers" for the ordinals the server StatefulSet does not run yet and the old list did not name: from the larger of the two up to `spec.topology.servers - 1`. A rewrite that adds none — a scale-down, or a scale-down set back while the drain held the replicas, whose list names servers that never stopped — is logged at V(1) and announces nothing. The StatefulSet is read from the cache; without one, the old list alone decides, as before.
- **why:** #619 S2, the v1.24.0 journey (2026-10-29): every set-back, refused or given back, rewrote the list from 3 servers to 5 and announced "Adding servers 3-4 as system secondaries" with an INFO "Scaled … from 3 to 5", while the replicas were held at 5 and nothing was added; the event's count reached 8.
- **pinned-by:** `TestScale_AnnouncesOnlyTheServersItAdds` (`internal/controller/scale_added_event_test.go`: 3 → 5 with 3 replicas, two servers announced; with 5 replicas, nothing; with 4, server 4), `TestScale_RestartDecision` (`internal/controller/scale_no_restart_test.go`). With the StatefulSet's replicas ignored under `go test -overlay`, the second and third cases fail.
- **enforcement:** unit test; not walked live yet.

### id 231 — A scale-down drain waits 20 s after its cordon before it deallocates, so a set-back can be given back
- **scope:** `DefaultScaleDownSettle`, `ScaleDownCordonedAtAnnotation`, `cordonSettle`, `settleMessage`, `setDrainAnnotations`, the cordon and DEALLOCATE steps of `reconcileScaleDownDrain`, `clearDrainRecords`, `giveBackCordoned` (`internal/controller/scale_down.go`); the reconciler's `ScaleDownSettle` and `clock`, and `Reconcile`'s wakes (`internal/controller/neo4jenterprisecluster_controller.go`, `internal/controller/pass_wake.go`); `ScaleDownSettle` in `cmd/main.go`; the scale-down section of `docs/user_guide/clustering.md`.
- **rule:** The drain's cordon step writes, in one merge patch with its record of the servers it cordons (id 224), when it cordons them: `neo4j.com/scale-down-cordoned-at`, RFC 3339 with nanoseconds (`cordonedAtLayout`; a time with no fraction, as the first builds wrote it truncated to the second, is read as the next second, `cordonedAt`, so the settle is never short; the message shows its end rounded up to the second). It deallocates a server the record lists only `ScaleDownSettle` after that — 20 s in production (`DefaultScaleDownSettle`; zero, the reconciler's default in tests, deallocates on the next pass as before). Meanwhile each pass asks to run again when the settle ends (`passWakes`), and `ServersPendingDrain` reads "Scale-down to 3 server(s) in progress: cordoned 2 server(s); their databases are deallocated from <time> unless spec.topology.servers is raised again before then". A set-back in that time gives back what the drain cordoned (`ENABLE SERVER`, id 224); after it, it is refused as before. The time goes with the record: when a set-back gives back the last recorded server, and when the drain ends. Servers cordoned by hand, which the drain deallocates without a cordon of its own, and a drain an earlier operator cordoned (no time recorded) have no settle. The time survives an operator restart. Every scale-down that cordons takes 20 s longer.
- **why:** #619, the v1.24.0 journey (2026-10-29): the give-back of #617 was not reached in three set-backs by hand. The drain's dry run, its cordon and, on the pass the cordon's own status write starts, its DEALLOCATE came within about a second of each other, so a set-back was nearly always too late, and refused. A cordon is the one step Neo4j can undo; a deallocating server cannot be enabled again.
- **pinned-by:** `TestScaleDownDrain_TheSettleKeepsASetBackRecoverable` (the cordon and its time, the message, the 20 s wake; nothing deallocated 0 s and 9 s on, an 11 s wake; set back 10 s after the scale-down: both enabled, one `ScaleDownSetBack`, the records and the time gone, "Scale-down not in progress"), `TestScaleDownDrain_TheSettleEndsAndTheDrainGoesOn` (deallocated once it ends; a set-back then is refused; lowered again, the drain ends and the time goes), `TestScaleDownDrain_NoSettleForServersCordonedByHand`, `TestPassWakes_BringTheNextPassForward`, `TestScaleDownDrain_TheSettleIsNeverShort` (a cordon at 16:28:18.7: nothing deallocated 19.5 s later, a 0.5 s wake, deallocated at 20 s, the message's end 16:28:39Z; a whole-second time from an earlier build: nothing deallocated 19.5 s after the cordon) (`internal/controller/scale_down_settle_test.go`). The walk of the #619 fixes measured a 19.3 s settle with the time truncated to the second; under `go test -overlay` the truncation back, the earlier build's time read as it is, and the end shown unrounded each fail `TestScaleDownDrain_TheSettleIsNeverShort`. Under `go test -overlay`, the settle switched off fails the first; the wake not noted fails the first two; `passWakes.apply` that changes nothing fails the last.
- **enforcement:** unit test; not walked live yet.

## Cross-cutting helpers referenced above

- **Condition helpers** (`internal/controller/conditions.go`): `SetReadyCondition` (~L65) is ONLY for the `Ready` condition type; use `SetNamedCondition` (~L88) for `ServersHealthy`/`DatabasesHealthy`/`PendingDependencies`. Pinned by `TestSetNamedCondition_Idempotent`.
- **Structured events** (`internal/controller/events.go`): event reasons (`EventReason*`) are defined here, together with some condition type/reason constants (`ConditionType*`, `ConditionReason*`, e.g. `ConditionTypePendingDependencies`); the rest of the condition constants (`ConditionTypeReady`, `ConditionTypeServersHealthy`, …) live in `conditions.go`. Use `corev1.EventTypeNormal` / `corev1.EventTypeWarning`, never raw reason strings.
- **Env-var ownership annotation** `neo4j.com/cluster-controller-env-vars` (`internal/controller/neo4jenterprisecluster_controller.go` `ownedEnvVarsAnnotation` ~L1156; see also `internal/controller/owned_keys.go`): the cluster controller records the env-var names it owns each reconcile so the next loop can enforce removals (`previously-owned ∖ desired`) via `mergeEnvVars` (~L1221) / `envVarsEqual` (~L1258) without disturbing foreign vars set by plugin/fleet/Aura controllers.
